## 4. Complete budget contract to freeze after policy review

Each row is necessary acceptance scope; unavailable policy/method blocks its dependent row, never narrows the original item.

| ID | Before source hypothesis / boundary | Required independent before and fixed oracle |
| --- | --- | --- |
| B01 | Same total/category limits displayed across two ledgers | A and B with distinct IDs and currencies, same category ID and different limits; edits/reloads never bleed. Switching back preserves exact values. |
| B02 | Budget amount and spent ignore period | Two adjacent months and same month across years; transaction on last/first local civil date, historical/future selection, no date substring ambiguity; only intended month contributes. |
| B03 | Carry-forward absent | Approved positive/negative/zero/unset policy, total/category consistency, month gaps and December→January; reopening/history edit recalculates or snapshots exactly as adopted. No choice assumed here. |
| B04 | Closed periods absent | Approved close/reopen and edit/delete/date-move semantics, explicit confirmation before mutation, cancellation preserves source, downstream chain trace/version and no duplicates. |
| B05 | Legacy scalar ambiguous | Exact old raw bytes and category budgets preserved until an approved user allocation/migration; value5000 alone never proves sample. Interrupted/denied migration cannot lose originals or publish half state. |
| B06 | Missing/corrupt reads seed5000 | Fresh live, new ledger/month, explicit demo, live exit from demo, absent/null/array/mixed/unsupported/nonfinite/denied source. No falsely empty/successful value; sample only visibly explicit example, no automatic write. |
| B07 | Total/category controls conflate values | Zero/unset/positive/negative/NaN/Infinity/blank/localized input plus bounds and currency precision; deterministic understandable error before write. Default5000 only explicit sample; user-set5000 stays valid. |
| B08 | Global category references | Rename/reorder/delete/recreate/same ID and revised identity; no remapping another period budget or previous carry. Finite category-total coherence per approved contract. |
| B09 | Transaction mutation impact | Create/edit/delete/backdate/move ledger/change category/change currency/prepay/transfer and recurring posting; only affected period amounts change, no double balance or duplicated carry. |
| B10 | Ledger/account lifecycle | New ledger, switch, currency edit, archive/delete/recreate and history references; preserve other-ledger/month bytes and BK-08 archive/history invariants. A destructive choice is not inferred. |
| B11 | Persistence/reopen | New-document reload, browser reopen, selected historical month, month/year/DST rollover, system-zone change; stable stored civil period and approved period clock, no mount normalization writes. |
| B12 | Failures and readonly source | Canonical serialization/quota/read denial, bad source, device mirror partial; draft retained, no false commit, retry/discard exact accounting and no duplicate mutation. Bad data never becomes writable seed. |
| B13 | Account/generation and pending | A→locked→B→A, demo↔live, replacement generation, deletion tombstone, stale UI/editor/export/FileReader; first visible and persistent outcome cannot leak or republish old data. |
| B14 | External changes and late work | Second document write/remove/corrupt, pending draft, event delivery, readFile completion, queued callbacks and URL cleanup; actual original request settled with source/owner/period identity, no stale continuation closing/reassigning current editor. |
| B15 | Export/import boundaries | Exact real draft/account download bytes under source denial and account changes, Blob/URL/click failure and cleanup; CSV remains transaction exchange and late import bound to captured ledger. No unsupported full restore. |
| B16 | Host departure/native UI | Real /app/bookkeeping, rail, CmdK, back/forward/direct route, signout/account management, tab/modal close while pending; honest current-mount-only draft guarantee unless separately authorized. |
| B17 | Visual/keyboard | EN/ZH light/dark at375/414/768/1024/1440 with recorded heights; all edited/empty/error/draft/demo/period states, 44px targets, no clipping, visible provenance/period/currency; trusted Tab/ShiftTab/Enter/Space/Escape exactly-once and per-stop focus pixels using qualified method. |
| B18 | All consumer/full regression | BudgetView, BudgetMini, category cards, dashboard/calendar/insight, canonical export/migration/account deletion, package original suites and complete canonical G1/affected callers. No changed caller or required early evidence omitted. |

Period is an entity identity, never transient tab index, display label, accountId alone or updatedAt. Parent ledger and account generation/epoch bind every operation; delete/recreate identity must not revive old work. Adopted calendar/time policy must explicitly settle the source for a month boundary; current local date helpers are a source fact, while changing global date ownership is prohibited. Clock timezone display preference does not implicitly set financial period time.

## 5. Persistence, source availability and dependency decisions

Bookkeeping bypasses the coordinated createScopedStorage.setItemAccount route: synchronous raw localStorage writes call accountScope.assertCurrent/physicalKey but do not acquire lifecycle locks or check a durable committed marker on every write. Name-scoping alone does not prove cross-tab generation/deletion safety. BK-07 entity schema/rollover cannot be admitted on a claim that an absent queue or fixed scalar tests already prove atomicity. Require a separately bounded writer/lifecycle impact covering public write/reset, hook, migration, canonical/device partial, external source and late file operations. Only actual acquired/pending product operations can prove lock behavior. BRD or shared-host proposals are not automatically adopted for BK.

Source availability requires a result that distinguishes valid absence from denied/corrupt/unsupported raw and a UI that refuses unsafe writes while preserving exact raw evidence. This intersects BK-01/REL-07/11, but that intersection cannot waive B06/B12 or authorize every related repair. Root must register finite impact/implementation paths for only the necessary dependency and retain all original item states. Likewise BK-08 lifecycle, REL-04 exports/ownership, REL-05 errors, REL-08 cross-tab and REL-09 durable draft boundaries remain distinct. No author may close those items transitively.

AccountDataGate remount key is kind/accountId/generation/epoch; actual gate changes revoke old callbacks before business content returns. Registration/account owner logic and export/deletion source must be frozen with fixtures; test activation of account scope is disclosed and cannot be advertised as hosted authentication. Demo namespace separation exists but createSeedBookkeepingState currently ignores mode, so namespace alone is not B06 completion.

Future accepted schema migration must preserve unknown legacy ownership, source bytes and nonbudget records; avoid in-place destructive normalize-on-read, reentrant storage spies, two unjoined writes and stale whole-state overwrite. Import/export and migration validators must reject unsupported schema explicitly. Old clients must be assessed before changing version2; no invented version number, global new key, cloud sync or migration implementation is authorized here.

**E24 rows** (each count is the AppRail final regression at `f9eb4b1`):
- **AppRail (new in r2):**
  - Sol eight modes through `../web-apprail-order-recovery-sol/verify-fixed.mjs` (`e944cb22…`): `bytes` 26, `domain` 31, `merge` 21, `drag` 18, `field` 24, `continuity-export` 22, `host` 33 and `original` 123;
  - parent host through `../web-apprail-order-recovery-independent/verify-fixed.mjs` (`646bf047…`): 31/31;
  - **C-RD1** 15/15, judging Features `downstream` case 014.
- **Appearance:**
  - Sol `bytes` 65, `fields` 89, `reset` 34, `queues` 56, `host` 33, `retry-all` 48 and `original` 187;
  - `continuity-export`: frozen 24/26 (006, 007), recorded, **and** the OE copy `../web-appearance-recovery-oracle-erratum/verify-erratum.mjs … corrected` 26/26, judging;
  - parent host 33; package 11 files / 137.
- **Features:**
  - Sol `bytes` 17, `fields` 49, `reset` 31, `queues` 40, `continuity-export` 26 and `original` 6;
  - `downstream` three ways: frozen 13/15 and C-FD1 14/15, both recorded, and C-RD1 15/15, judging;
  - host 40; package 7 files / 45; reader tests 5 files / 17.
- **More:**
  - Sol `fields` 22, `reset` 20, `queues` 14 and `owner-export` 13; `original` 15; host 11;
  - `boundaries`: frozen, recorded as it falls, **and** the C-FB002 copy `../web-more-recovery-fb002/verify-fb002.mjs … corrected full` 10/10, judging.
- **Others:**
  - Sticky: Sol 109, original 10, host 28;
  - Notifications: Sol 41, Astra boundaries 24, Astra host 15, parent host 12;
  - Date & Time 7;
  - the Smart Lists, Collaborate and Pomodoro host suites through the accepted buffer copies in `../web-apprail-order-recovery-final/host-suites/`, with the per-mode counts of that receipt's §6;
  - settings-shell 11 files / 54; settings-rest 44 files / 314.

**Rules.**
- E1–E5 must be committed before Terra starts.
- E25 is produced last and enumerates every other item.
- A later item cannot substitute for a missing earlier one.
- Acceptance re-derives at least one hash per item and BLOCKS on any absent ID.
- **Judging copies.** The frozen More `boundaries`, Features `downstream` (cases 012 and 014) and Appearance `continuity-export` (cases 006 and 007) failures are judged by their copies: C-FB002, C-RD1 and OE. C-FD1 runs beside them with its predicted outcome and judges nothing. A judging copy that fails is a regression.
- **K-1 and transport.** Every native runner written for this caller uses pipe transport, sends no `nativeVirtualKeyCode` and audits keys (K-1). Reused frozen runners that send none run unchanged, with their frozen transport (AppRail acceptance §6 item 10).
- **Drags.** Native drags use trusted CDP input only (§12 rule 14).
- **Product-delta preconditions.** A reused frozen runner may refuse at a precondition bound to an earlier caller's product delta. The verifier then commits that refusal log, and runs a copy that replaces only that precondition with a stricter one naming this caller's §11 delta. This follows the Appearance and AppRail E24/E25 precedents, and the deviation is disclosed for acceptance. Every other precondition and assertion stays unchanged.
- **Capacity copies (pre-registered; AppRail acceptance §6 item 10).**
  - **When.** A reused frozen runner aborts with `ENOBUFS` (or any buffer-capacity error) while reading `git archive`, before any test runs.
  - **What the verifier does.** It commits the refusal transcript. It then runs a copy that differs from the frozen runner only in the archive buffer (sized from the measured archive, at least twice its size, or replaced by streaming) and, if the copy lives in a deeper directory, in its `root` depth, plus a header comment.
  - **Requirements.** The copy's diff against the frozen runner is committed. Staged test and fixture files must be byte-identical to the frozen ones. Counts must equal the accepted receipts and the `f9eb4b1` control.
  - **Pre-registered for:** the four Dashboard Header runners (E17), whose 100 MiB buffer is below the `f9eb4b1` archive.
  - **Applies on refusal to:** the three 200 MiB F1 runners `verify-f1.mjs`, `verify-f1-callers.mjs` and `verify-f1-race.mjs` (E15). Their buffer, 209,715,200 bytes, exceeds the 173,905,920-byte archive at the r2 docs head, but later evidence may outgrow it.
  - **Status.** The copy procedure is not a contract revision and needs no separate ruling batch; acceptance reviews each copy.
- **Not rerun.** The Features native host and downstream suites (Features E12/E13) and the AppRail native E9–E14 are not rerun. Their subjects (Features rail departures and drags, and AppRail's own surfaces) are outside the Dashboard packages, and the empty shell, `App.tsx` and tokens diff (E19) plus the `.app-rail`/`.topbar` invariance (E12) bound them. If either bound fails, they must be rerun.

## Appendix F — original authorized goal verbatim

你接任 XAI_Desktop 全产品 312 项审计的唯一总控（controller）。Claude 总控窗口已于 2026-10-09 暂停并把全部后续工作交给你（A-Codex 模式）。

## 仓库与固定点
- 仓库：/Users/lijinlong/Desktop/Jinlong_Project/Coding_project/AI_Desktop/XAI_Desktop
- 控制分支：codex/web/full-product-audit-20260908；交接时 HEAD = b5688d8（已 push，与 origin 0 0）
- 当前产品基线：f9eb4b1f207bc4b46f547b90afc250424b3c8695（AppRail caller 已接受后的产品头）
- 正式统计：13/312 completed、299 未关闭。caller 被接受不改变这个统计；只追加证据，不改任何条目状态。

## 先读（按顺序）
1. AGENTS.md、CLAUDE.md、docs/workflow/project/workflow.md、docs/workflow/project/multi-machine-development.md
2. docs/reviews/20260908-full-product-audit/CURRENT-CONTROL-PLANE.md：唯一入口。重点看以下几节：
   - 已接受的调用方
   - 当前进行中的调用方（CP-CLOCK-01 全部行、总控确认与各批裁定）
   - 交接记录（2026-10-09）
   - 执行者映射
   - 成本检查
   - 本轮唯一任务（批次 70）
3. docs/reviews/web-dashboard-clock-recovery-contract/contract.md：时钟合同 r2，提交 8bf6139，SHA-256 214dc758…

## 硬规则（必须遵守）
- **总控只做六件事：** 读、排程、核对、接收提交、同步状态、启动独立任务。任何产品修复、verifier 实现或测试修复，都必须在新的独立窗口/worktree 中做；禁止在总控窗口直接修。
- **批次：** 一次只做一个有界批次。每批写明以下各项：
  - 固定 SHA、范围、验收条件；
  - 禁止项、成本上限（每个运行单元诊断迭代 ≤3 轮，开发探测不计入但须披露）；
  - 停止条件。
- **角色：**
  - Astra 负责合同与最终 acceptance，Luna 负责低风险检索，Terra 负责实施，Sol 负责验证；
  - reviewer 永不修复；
  - 每个独立执行者都不能是同一 caller 前面批次的作者。
- **真实产品失败：** 冻结复现、影响范围与正确 oracle，然后另开修复窗口修。
- **不得混淆：** 测试通过、caller 接受、文档、部署就绪、历史验证是不同的事。
- **禁止自动操作：** 不自动 merge/rebase，不做分支提升、部署、发布或 Web→Desktop 同步。不得改 dev/main/web。
- **接收流程（每批）：**
  1. 核对父提交、只新增或只改允许路径、执行者 worktree clean、回执 hash 覆盖、日志计数与 PRECONDITION，并抽查 hash 与截图；
  2. 用 `git cherry-pick --ff <sha>` 快进接收，然后 push；
  3. 用 `git merge-base --is-ancestor` 确认已在 origin；
  4. 清理执行者 worktree 与临时分支；
  5. 跑 `pnpm git:sync-check -- --fetch`，正常结果是 failures=0、warnings=1。
- **控制面与台账：**
  - 控制面每批更新一次：本轮模式、HEAD、CP 行、本会话提交表、本轮唯一任务、下一步。写文件的脚本必须先读取并校验完，再写。
  - 台账（ALL-TODO-CURRENT.md、EXECUTION.md、EXECUTION.json）只在 caller 接受后对账，只追加证据，并校验状态计数不变。
- **提交：**
  - 只 stage 精确路径；
  - 格式为 `type(scope): summary`，正文含 Why/What/Scope/Risk/Docs/Tests；
  - 每批以 commit → push → sync-check 结束。
- **成本：** 每三批记一次成本周期，不阻断。最近一期记录的是周期 20（批次 62–64）；下一期覆盖批次 65–67（acceptance、库存刷新、时钟合同 r2）。
- **执行窗口：**
  - 只用 worktree 内自己启动的 server；
  - 禁止在主检出启动 dev server、使用 preview 工具或写入主检出；主检出只能作只读的依赖根（XAI_DEPS_ROOT）。
- **evidence runner 惯例：**
  - 不可变 git archive，必须流式读取（archive 已约 148MB，超过旧的 100 MiB 缓冲）；
  - lockfile gate 值为 df05f2dd…；
  - @repo 固定到 archive 并带守卫；记录 requested 与 resolved SHA；拒绝覆盖；保留非零退出码。
- **native runner 惯例：**
  - headless Chrome 经 CDP pipe transport；
  - 绝不发送 nativeVirtualKeyCode（K-1），保留被动按键审计；
  - 拖动只用可信 CDP 输入并记录 isTrusted；
  - 焦点可见性用批次 48 冻结的 pixelFocusWalk（来源 bacdbbc:docs/reviews/web-appearance-recovery-native/verify-visual-keyboard-5bbf473.mjs），断言其身份 hash。
- **回归：** 必须同时运行全部判定副本：C-FB002（More boundaries）、OE（Appearance continuity-export）、C-RD1（Features downstream case 014）；C-FD1 按预测记录。最终回归按 G1 列全合同 Required evidence 的每一项。
- **停止条件：**
  - 证据与合同矛盾；
  - 存在真实产品失败但没有开修复窗口；
  - 出现未知的 dirty worktree；
  - 结果不可复现；
  - 将越过模块、部署、发布或台账授权边界。
  遇到时停下并向用户报告。
- **每轮向用户汇报：**
  - 分支、SHA、worktree；
  - PASS/FAIL/BLOCKED；
  - 证据与未验证范围；
  - 提交 SHA、push 与 sync-check 结果；
  - 312 统计是否变化及原因；
  - 下一个单一批次；
  - 启动了哪些独立窗口。
